+    "scram_sha256_verifier",
+    "secrets_path",
+    "tenant_compose_env",
+    "tenant_slug",
+    "write_secrets",
+]
+
+AGE_CONTAINER = "dopemux-postgres-age"
+SUPERUSER = "dopemux_age"
+SHARED_DB = "dopemux_knowledge_graph"
+UNREACHABLE_EVIDENCE = "postgres unreachable"
+UNKNOWN_HISTORY = "CONPORT_TENANT_UNKNOWN_HISTORY"
+ENVRC_FILENAME = ".envrc.dopemux-mcp"
+SECRETS_FILENAME = "secrets.env"
+STATE_FILENAME = "conport-tenant.state"
+STATE_DATABASE = "database"
+STATE_GATE = "gate"
+CRED_KEYS = ("CONPORT_DB_NAME", "CONPORT_DB_USER", "CONPORT_DB_PASSWORD")
+GATE_SCRIPT = "migrations/conport_migration_gate.py"
+GATE_APPLY_ENV = "DPMX_CONPORT_MIGRATION_APPLY"
+
+# Workspace-scoped tables of the sealed schema.sql (each has workspace_id).
+WORKSPACE_TABLES = (
+    "workspace_contexts",
+    "decisions",
+    "progress_entries",
+    "session_snapshots",
+    "custom_data",
+    "entity_relationships",
+    "search_cache",
+)
+
+# psql-then-adopt: every migration the sealed gate requires (its
+# REQUIRED_MIGRATIONS), applied with psql before the gate runs. The script
+# predates 008; leaving 008 to the gate would run psql inside the gate's own
+# open transaction, which 008's header documents as a deadlock on a fresh DB.
+PROVISION_MIGRATIONS = (
+    "001_enhanced_decision_model",
+    "002_decision_patterns_table",
+    "003_multi_tenancy_foundation",
+    "004_unified_query_indexes",
+    "007_worktree_support_simple",
+    "008_write_principal",
+)
+
+Source = Literal["secrets", "legacy_envrc", "provisioned", "legacy_shared"]
+PsqlRunner = Callable[[str, str], subprocess.CompletedProcess]
+
+_SLUG_RE = re.compile(r"^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,40}$")
+_IDENT_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$")
+_HEX_PASSWORD_RE = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{32,}$")
+_SAFE_VALUE_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9_.,:/@%+=-]*$")
+
+
+class TenantError(RuntimeError):
+    """A tenant failure with a finding code; the text never carries a password."""
+
+    def __init__(self, code: str, detail: str = "") -> None:
+        super().__init__(f"{code}: {detail}" if detail else code)
+        self.code = code
+        self.detail = detail
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class TenantCredentials:
+    db_name: str
+    db_user: str
+    db_password: str = field(repr=False)
+    source: Source = "secrets"
+    evidence: str = ""
+    gate_pending: bool = False
+
+
+# --- paths -----------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def secrets_path(project_id: str, *, secrets_root: Optional[Path] = None) -> Path:
+    """``<runtime parent>/projects/<project_id>/secrets.env``; ``secrets_root`` replaces ``.../projects``."""
+    base = project_dir(project_id)  # validates the id even when a root is given
+    if secrets_root is not None:
+        base = Path(secrets_root) / str(project_id)
+    return base / SECRETS_FILENAME
+
+
+def _state_path(secrets_file: Path) -> Path:
+    return Path(secrets_file).parent / STATE_FILENAME
+
+
+def schema_path() -> Path:
+    return (
+        dr.product_root() / "docker" / "mcp-servers-source" / "conport" / "schema.sql"
+    )
+
+
+def migrations_dir() -> Path:
+    return schema_path().parent / "migrations"
+
+
+# --- secrets file ----------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def _format_value(value: str) -> str:
+    if any(ch in value for ch in ("\n", "\r", "\x00")):
+        raise TenantError(
+            "CONPORT_TENANT_SECRETS_INVALID", "credential values must be one line"
+        )
+    if _SAFE_VALUE_RE.match(value):
+        return value
+    if "'" not in value:
+        return f"'{value}'"
+    return '"' + value.replace("\\", "\\\\").replace('"', '\\"') + '"'
+
+
+def _parse_value(raw: str) -> str:
+    raw = raw.strip()
+    if len(raw) >= 2 and raw[0] == raw[-1] == "'":
+        return raw[1:-1]
+    if len(raw) >= 2 and raw[0] == raw[-1] == '"':
+        return re.sub(r"\\(.)", r"\1", raw[1:-1])
+    return raw
+
+
+def _atomic_write(path: Path, text: str) -> None:
+    """Temp file in the same directory, 0600, fsync, ``os.replace``."""
+    path = Path(path)
+    _mkdir_private(path.parent)
+    os.chmod(path.parent, 0o700)
+    fd, tmp = tempfile.mkstemp(
+        prefix=f".{path.name}.", suffix=".tmp", dir=str(path.parent)
+    )
+    try:
+        os.fchmod(fd, 0o600)
+        with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
+            fh.write(text)
+            fh.flush()
+            os.fsync(fh.fileno())
+        os.replace(tmp, path)
+        os.chmod(path, 0o600)
+    except BaseException:
+        with contextlib.suppress(FileNotFoundError):
+            os.unlink(tmp)
+        raise
+
+
+def write_secrets(path: Path, creds: TenantCredentials) -> None:
+    """``KEY=VALUE`` lines for the three ``CONPORT_DB_*`` keys; dir 0700, file 0600, atomic."""
+    values = (creds.db_name, creds.db_user, creds.db_password)
+    body = "".join(
+        f"{key}={_format_value(val)}\n" for key, val in zip(CRED_KEYS, values)
+    )
+    _atomic_write(Path(path), body)
+
+
+def load_secrets(path: Path) -> Optional[TenantCredentials]:
+    """None when the file is absent; ``TenantError`` when present but unusable."""
+    path = Path(path)
+    if not path.exists():
+        return None
+    remedy = (
+        f"{path} is unusable; restore the file from backup or ask the operator "
+        "(without it the existing tenant role cannot be reused)"
+    )
+    try:
+        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+    except OSError as exc:
+        raise TenantError(
+            "CONPORT_TENANT_SECRETS_INVALID", f"{remedy} ({exc.__class__.__name__})"
+        ) from None
+    values: Dict[str, str] = {}
+    for line in text.splitlines():
+        line = line.strip()
+        if not line or line.startswith("#"):
+            continue
+        if line.startswith("export "):
+            line = line[len("export ") :].lstrip()
+        key, sep, raw = line.partition("=")
+        if sep:
+            values[key.strip()] = _parse_value(raw)
+    if not all(values.get(key) for key in CRED_KEYS):
+        raise TenantError(
+            "CONPORT_TENANT_SECRETS_INVALID", f"{remedy} (needs {', '.join(CRED_KEYS)})"
+        )
+    return TenantCredentials(
+        db_name=values["CONPORT_DB_NAME"],
+        db_user=values["CONPORT_DB_USER"],
+        db_password=values["CONPORT_DB_PASSWORD"],
+        source="secrets",
+    )
+
+
+def os_error_detail(exc: BaseException) -> str:
+    """Class, strerror and filename(s) of an OSError (a ValueError's message); never file contents."""
+    if isinstance(exc, OSError):
+        parts = [exc.__class__.__name__]
+        parts += [str(v) for v in (exc.strerror, exc.filename, exc.filename2) if v]
+        return ": ".join(parts)
+    return f"{exc.__class__.__name__}: {exc}"
+
+
+def _read_state(secrets_file: Path) -> str:
+    """The owed phase; "" when there is no state file. Unreadable is never "nothing owed"."""
+    path = _state_path(secrets_file)
+    try:
+        return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
+    except FileNotFoundError:
+        return ""
+    except OSError as exc:
+        raise TenantError(
+            "TENANT_STATE_UNREADABLE",
+            f"{path} cannot be read ({os_error_detail(exc)}); fix its permissions or restore "
+            "it, or ask the operator",
+        ) from None
+
+
+def _database_progress(state: str) -> Optional[str]:
+    """None unless the database phase is owed; "" before any migration, else the last applied."""
+    if state == STATE_DATABASE:
+        return ""
+    if state.startswith(STATE_DATABASE + ":"):
+        return state[len(STATE_DATABASE) + 1 :]
+    return None
+
+
+def _remaining_migrations(applied_through: str) -> Tuple[str, ...]:
+    if not applied_through:
+        return PROVISION_MIGRATIONS
+    if applied_through not in PROVISION_MIGRATIONS:
+        raise TenantError(
+            "CONPORT_TENANT_PROVISION_FAILED",
+            f"recorded migration {applied_through!r} is not one this provisioner applies",
+        )
+    return PROVISION_MIGRATIONS[PROVISION_MIGRATIONS.index(applied_through) + 1 :]
+
+
+def _write_state(secrets_file: Path, state: str) -> None:
+    _atomic_write(_state_path(secrets_file), state + "\n")
+
+
+def _clear_state(secrets_file: Path) -> None:
+    with contextlib.suppress(FileNotFoundError):
+        _state_path(secrets_file).unlink()
+
+
+def pending_gate_credentials(
+    project_id: str, *, secrets_root: Optional[Path] = None
+) -> Optional[TenantCredentials]:
+    """Credentials whose migration gate is still owed (no psql call)."""
+    path = secrets_path(project_id, secrets_root=secrets_root)
+    if _read_state(path) != STATE_GATE:
+        return None
+    creds = load_secrets(path)
+    return replace(creds, gate_pending=True) if creds is not None else None
+
+
+def mark_gate_done(project_id: str, *, secrets_root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
+    _clear_state(secrets_path(project_id, secrets_root=secrets_root))
+
+
+def tenant_compose_env(creds: Optional[TenantCredentials]) -> Dict[str, str]:
+    """``CONPORT_DB_*`` for one ``compose up``; empty for the shared store (compose defaults)."""
+    if creds is None or creds.source == "legacy_shared" or not creds.db_password:
+        return {}
+    return dict(zip(CRED_KEYS, (creds.db_name, creds.db_user, creds.db_password)))
+
+
+# --- psql ------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def psql_command(database: str) -> List[str]:
+    if not _IDENT_RE.match(database):
+        raise ValueError(f"unsafe database name: {database!r}")
+    return [
+        "docker", "exec", "-i", AGE_CONTAINER,
+        "psql", "-v", "ON_ERROR_STOP=1", "-U", SUPERUSER, "-d", database,
+    ]  # fmt: skip
+
+
+def _hermetic_refusal() -> bool:
+    """Under pytest a default (real) runner is refused: tests inject fakes."""
+    return bool(os.environ.get("PYTEST_CURRENT_TEST"))
+
+
+def default_psql_runner(
+    runner: Optional[dr.Runner] = None, *, timeout: float = 120.0
+) -> PsqlRunner:
+    """``(database, sql)`` as the bootstrap superuser; the SQL goes to psql's stdin."""
+
+    def _psql(database: str, sql: str) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
+        argv = psql_command(database)
+        if runner is None and _hermetic_refusal():
+            return subprocess.CompletedProcess(
+                argv,
+                1,
+                "",
+                "refused: a real psql under pytest needs an injected runner",
+            )
+        res = dr.run_cmd(argv, runner=runner, input_text=sql, timeout=timeout)
+        return subprocess.CompletedProcess(argv, res.exit_code, res.stdout, res.stderr)
+
+    return _psql
+
+
+def _marker(result: subprocess.CompletedProcess, name: str) -> Optional[str]:
+    match = re.search(rf"\b{name}=([^\s|]*)", str(result.stdout or ""))
+    return match.group(1) if match else None
+
+
+def _literal(value: str) -> str:
+    if "\x00" in value:
+        raise ValueError("NUL in SQL literal")
+    return "'" + value.replace("'", "''") + "'"
+
+
+SCRAM_ITERATIONS = 4096
+SCRAM_SALT_BYTES = 16
+
+
+def scram_sha256_verifier(
+    password: str, *, salt: Optional[bytes] = None, iterations: int = SCRAM_ITERATIONS
+) -> str:
+    """Postgres ``SCRAM-SHA-256$<iter>:<salt>$<StoredKey>:<ServerKey>`` (RFC 5802/7677).
+
+    ``CREATE ROLE ... PASSWORD '<verifier>'`` stores it as is, so the plaintext
+    never reaches the server. ``password`` must already be SASLprep-normalised
+    (provisioning only uses generated hex). ``salt`` defaults to 16 random bytes.
+    """
+    salt = secrets.token_bytes(SCRAM_SALT_BYTES) if salt is None else salt
+    salted = hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac("sha256", password.encode("utf-8"), salt, iterations)
+    client_key = hmac.new(salted, b"Client Key", "sha256").digest()
+    stored_key = hashlib.sha256(client_key).digest()
+    server_key = hmac.new(salted, b"Server Key", "sha256").digest()
+
+    def b64(raw: bytes) -> str:
+        return base64.b64encode(raw).decode("ascii")
+
+    return f"SCRAM-SHA-256${iterations}:{b64(salt)}${b64(stored_key)}:{b64(server_key)}"
+
+
+def _redact(text: str, secret: str) -> str:
+    if not secret:
+        return text
+    for form in {secret, quote(secret, safe="")}:
+        text = text.replace(form, "***")
+    return text
+
+
+#: ``scheme://user:password@``: the password of any URL is masked.
+_URL_PASSWORD_RE = re.compile(r"([A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9+.-]*://[^\s:/@]+:)([^\s@/]+)(@)")
+
+
+def _redacted_tail(
+    text: str, secrets_: Sequence[str], limit: Optional[int] = 300
+) -> str:
+    """The last ``limit`` characters of ``text`` with every secret replaced first.
+
+    Each value in ``secrets_`` (and its URL-quoted form) and the password of any
+    ``scheme://user:password@`` URL become ``***``. Redacting after the cut would
+    miss a secret the cut split, leaving a fragment. ``limit=None`` keeps it all.
+    """
+    clean = str(text or "").strip()
+    for secret in secrets_:
+        clean = _redact(clean, secret)
+    clean = _URL_PASSWORD_RE.sub(r"\1***\3", clean)
+    return clean if limit is None else clean[-limit:]
+
+
+#: Shared with the launcher's start-failure text.
+redacted_tail = _redacted_tail
+
+
+# --- legacy history in the shared store ---------------------------------------------
+
+
+def legacy_workspace_aliases(identity: Any) -> List[str]:
+    """``project_root``, its basename and every ``git worktree list`` path."""
+    root = str(getattr(identity, "project_root", "") or "")
+    aliases: List[str] = []
+
+    def add(value: str) -> None:
+        if value and value not in aliases:
+            aliases.append(value)
+
+    add(root)
+    add(Path(root).name if root else "")
+    if root:
+        try:
+            proc = subprocess.run(
+                ["git", "-C", root, "worktree", "list", "--porcelain"],
+                capture_output=True,
+                text=True,
+                timeout=10,
+                check=False,
+            )
+        except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
+            proc = None
+        if proc is not None and proc.returncode == 0:
+            for line in proc.stdout.splitlines():
+                if line.startswith("worktree "):
+                    add(line[len("worktree ") :].strip())
+    return aliases
+
+
+_TABLES_SQL = (
+    "SELECT 'DPMX_TABLES=' || coalesce(string_agg(c.relname::text, ',' ORDER BY c.relname), '')\n"
+    "FROM pg_catalog.pg_class c\n"
+    "JOIN pg_catalog.pg_namespace n ON n.oid = c.relnamespace\n"
+    "WHERE n.nspname = 'public' AND c.relkind IN ('r', 'p')\n"
+    "  AND c.relname IN (" + ", ".join(_literal(t) for t in WORKSPACE_TABLES) + ");\n"
+)
+
+
+def count_legacy_rows(aliases: Sequence[str], *, psql: PsqlRunner) -> Optional[int]:
+    """Rows for ``aliases`` across the shared store's workspace tables; None when psql fails.
+
+    Tables the shared database never got (a host whose shared conport never
+    ran) count as zero rows rather than as an unreachable server.
+    """
+    names = [a for a in aliases if a]
+    if not names:
+        return 0
+    try:
+        listed = psql(SHARED_DB, _TABLES_SQL)
+        if listed.returncode != 0:
+            return None
+        found = _marker(listed, "DPMX_TABLES")
+        if found is None:
+            return None
+        existing = set(filter(None, found.split(",")))
+        present = [t for t in WORKSPACE_TABLES if t in existing]
+        if not present:
+            return 0
+        in_list = ", ".join(_literal(a) for a in names)
+        union = "\n    UNION ALL\n    ".join(
+            f"SELECT 1 FROM public.{t} WHERE workspace_id IN ({in_list})"
+            for t in present
+        )
+        result = psql(
+            SHARED_DB,
+            f"SELECT 'DPMX_COUNT=' || count(*) FROM (\n    {union}\n) AS legacy;\n",
+        )
+        if result.returncode != 0:
+            return None
+        value = _marker(result, "DPMX_COUNT")
+        return int(value) if value and value.isdigit() else None
+    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - any failure means "unknown", never a raise
+        return None
+
+
+# --- provisioning (database phase) ----------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def tenant_slug(identity: Any) -> str:
+    """``<basename>_<project hash[:8]>``: unique per project root, SQL-identifier safe."""
+    root = str(getattr(identity, "project_root", "") or "")
+    base = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", Path(root).name.lower()).strip("_") or "workspace"
+    if not base[0].isalpha():
+        base = f"p_{base}"
+    base = base[:24].rstrip("_")
+    digest = str(getattr(identity, "project_hash", "") or "").lower()
+    if not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", digest):
+        digest = hashlib.sha256(root.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
+    slug = f"{base}_{digest[:8]}"
+    if not _SLUG_RE.match(slug):
+        raise TenantError(
+            "CONPORT_TENANT_PROVISION_FAILED", f"cannot derive a tenant slug for {root}"
+        )
+    return slug
+
+
+def filtered_schema(path: Path) -> str:
+    """schema.sql without its sample-data block (the script's awk filter)."""
+    out: List[str] = []
+    skipping = False
+    for line in Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines(keepends=True):
+        if line.startswith("-- INITIAL DATA MIGRATION"):
+            skipping = True
+        if line.startswith("-- Grant permissions to the ConPort"):
+            skipping = False
+        if not skipping:
+            out.append(line)
+    sql = "".join(out)
+    if re.search(r"^INSERT INTO", sql, re.M):
+        raise TenantError(
+            "CONPORT_TENANT_PROVISION_FAILED",
+            "schema seed filter left INSERT statements",
+        )
+    return sql
+
+
+def _workspace_rows_sql(workspace_id: str, db: str) -> str:
+    ws = _literal(workspace_id)
+    desc = _literal(f"Canonical workspace for {db} (project-walled {db})")
+    return (
+        "INSERT INTO users (id, email, display_name)\n"
+        "VALUES ('default', 'default@localhost', 'Default User')\n"
+        "ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING;\n"
+        "INSERT INTO workspaces (id, owner_user_id, name, description)\n"
+        f"VALUES ({ws}, 'default', {ws}, {desc})\n"
+        "ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING;\n"
+        "INSERT INTO user_workspace_access (user_id, workspace_id, role)\n"
+        f"VALUES ('default', {ws}, 'owner')\n"
+        "ON CONFLICT (user_id, workspace_id) DO NOTHING;\n"
+    )
+
+
+_VERIFY_SQL = (
+    "SELECT 'DPMX_VERIFY='"
+    " || (SELECT count(*) FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_schema = 'public')"
+    " || ',' || (SELECT count(*) FROM decisions)"
+    " || ',' || (SELECT count(*) FROM progress_entries)"
+    " || ',' || (SELECT count(*) FROM workspaces);\n"
+)
+
+
+def provision_database(
+    *,
+    slug: str,
+    workspace_id: str,
+    password: str,
+    psql: PsqlRunner,
+    schema_path: Path,
+    migrations_dir: Path,
+    resume: bool = False,
+    persist: Optional[Callable[[], None]] = None,
+    applied_through: str = "",
+    on_migration: Optional[Callable[[str], None]] = None,
+) -> None:
+    """The script's database phase: role, database, wall, extensions, schema, migrations, rows, checks.
+
+    A role that already exists raises ``ROLE_EXISTS_WITHOUT_SECRETS`` (never
+    ``ALTER ROLE ... PASSWORD``) unless ``resume`` says this run's own secrets
+    file already holds its password. ``persist`` runs after that check and
+    before ``CREATE ROLE``, so the password is on disk before Postgres has it.
+    When the schema already exists, only the migrations after
+    ``applied_through`` run (001/002 are not re-runnable); ``on_migration`` is
+    told each one as soon as it has applied, so progress survives a kill.
+    """
+    if not _SLUG_RE.match(slug):
+        raise TenantError(
+            "CONPORT_TENANT_PROVISION_FAILED", f"unsafe tenant slug {slug!r}"
+        )
+    if not _HEX_PASSWORD_RE.match(password):
+        raise TenantError(
+            "CONPORT_TENANT_PROVISION_FAILED", "tenant password must be generated hex"
+        )
+    role = db = f"conport_{slug}"
+    as_role = f"SET ROLE {role};\n"
+    hidden = [password]  # plus the SCRAM verifier once there is one
+
+    def run(database: str, sql: str, what: str) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
+        try:
+            res = psql(database, sql)
+        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
+            raise TenantError(
+                "CONPORT_TENANT_PROVISION_FAILED", f"{what}: {exc.__class__.__name__}"
+            ) from None
+        if res.returncode != 0:
+            tail = _redacted_tail(str(res.stderr or res.stdout or ""), hidden)
+            raise TenantError(
+                "CONPORT_TENANT_PROVISION_FAILED",
+                f"{what} failed (psql exit {res.returncode}): {tail}",
+            )
+        return res
+
+    def scalar(database: str, sql: str, name: str, what: str) -> str:
+        value = _marker(run(database, sql, what), name)
+        if value is None:
+            raise TenantError(
+                "CONPORT_TENANT_PROVISION_FAILED", f"{what}: unexpected psql output"
+            )
+        return value
+
+    # 1. role + database (superuser); the wall: no PUBLIC connect
+    role_sql = f"SELECT 'DPMX_ROLE=' || count(*) FROM pg_catalog.pg_roles WHERE rolname = {_literal(role)};"
+    role_exists = scalar("postgres", role_sql, "DPMX_ROLE", "role lookup") != "0"
+    if role_exists and not resume:
+        raise TenantError(
+            "ROLE_EXISTS_WITHOUT_SECRETS",
+            f"role {role} already exists but this project has no secrets file for it; restore "
+            f"that file, or have an operator drop role and database {db} "
+            "(provisioning never resets a password)",
+        )
+    if persist is not None:
+        persist()
+    if not role_exists:
+        # A SCRAM verifier, never the plaintext: a failing statement is logged verbatim.
+        verifier = scram_sha256_verifier(password)
+        hidden.append(verifier)
+        run(
+            "postgres",
+            f"CREATE ROLE {role} LOGIN PASSWORD {_literal(verifier)};",
+            "CREATE ROLE",
+        )
+    db_sql = f"SELECT 'DPMX_DB=' || count(*) FROM pg_catalog.pg_database WHERE datname = {_literal(db)};"
+    if scalar("postgres", db_sql, "DPMX_DB", "database lookup") == "0":
+        run("postgres", f"CREATE DATABASE {db} OWNER {role};", "CREATE DATABASE")
+    run(
+        "postgres",
+        f"REVOKE CONNECT ON DATABASE {db} FROM PUBLIC;\nGRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON DATABASE {db} TO {role};\n",
+        "REVOKE CONNECT",
+    )
+    # 2. extensions (superuser-only); AGE is optional, as in the script
+    run(
+        db,
+        'CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "uuid-ossp";\nCREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "pg_trgm";\n',
+        "extensions",
+    )
+    with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
+        psql(db, "CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS age;\n")
+    # 3. + 4. filtered schema and SQL migrations AS the role (it owns its tables);
+    # skipped when a previous run already created them (schema.sql is not idempotent)
+    probe = (
+        as_role
+        + "SELECT 'DPMX_PRESENT=' || (to_regclass('public.decisions') IS NOT NULL)::text;\n"
+    )
+    present = scalar(db, probe, "DPMX_PRESENT", "schema probe") == "true"
+    todo: Tuple[str, ...]
+    if not present:
+        run(
+            db,
+            as_role + "BEGIN;\n" + filtered_schema(schema_path) + "\nCOMMIT;\n",
+            "schema",
+        )
+        todo = PROVISION_MIGRATIONS
+    else:
+        todo = _remaining_migrations(applied_through)
+    for name in todo:
+        sql = (Path(migrations_dir) / f"{name}.sql").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+        run(db, as_role + sql, f"migration {name}")
+        if on_migration is not None:
+            on_migration(name)
+    # 5. canonical workspace rows (real FKs to users)
+    run(db, as_role + _workspace_rows_sql(workspace_id, db), "workspace registration")
+    # 6. verification
+    counts = scalar(db, as_role + _VERIFY_SQL, "DPMX_VERIFY", "verification").split(",")
+    try:
+        _tables, decisions, progress, workspaces = (int(c) for c in counts)
+    except ValueError:
+        raise TenantError(
+            "CONPORT_TENANT_PROVISION_FAILED", "verification: unexpected counts"
+        ) from None
+    if not present and (decisions or progress):
+        raise TenantError(
+            "CONPORT_TENANT_PROVISION_FAILED",
+            "verification: seed rows leaked into the tenant",
+        )
+    if workspaces < 1:
+        raise TenantError(
+            "CONPORT_TENANT_PROVISION_FAILED", "verification: workspace not registered"
+        )
+
+
+# --- gate phase ---------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def _gate_database_url(creds: TenantCredentials) -> str:
+    user, pw, db = (
+        quote(v, safe="") for v in (creds.db_user, creds.db_password, creds.db_name)
+    )
+    return f"postgresql://{user}:{pw}@{AGE_CONTAINER}:5432/{db}"
+
+
+def adopt_with_gate(
+    container_name: str,
+    creds: TenantCredentials,
+    *,
+    runner: Optional[dr.Runner] = None,
+    timeout: float = 180.0,
+) -> None:
+    """``conport_migration_gate.py apply`` (adopts the psql-applied SQL), then ``verify``.
+
+    The gate reads ``DATABASE_URL`` from its environment, so the URL goes in a
+    0600 temp env file (``docker exec --env-file``) and argv carries only the
+    file's path. The file is removed in ``finally``.
+    """
+    if creds.source == "legacy_shared" or not creds.db_password:
+        raise TenantError(
+            "CONPORT_TENANT_GATE_FAILED", "no tenant credentials to adopt"
+        )
+    if runner is None and _hermetic_refusal():
+        raise TenantError(
+            "CONPORT_TENANT_GATE_FAILED",
+            "refused: a real docker exec under pytest needs an injected runner",
+        )
+    fd, tmp = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix="dopemux-conport-gate-", suffix=".env")
+    try:
+        os.fchmod(fd, 0o600)
+        with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
+            fh.write(f"DATABASE_URL={_gate_database_url(creds)}\n")
+        for command in ("apply", "verify"):
+            argv = ["docker", "exec", "--env-file", tmp]
+            if command == "apply":
+                argv += ["-e", f"{GATE_APPLY_ENV}=1"]
+            argv += [container_name, "python", GATE_SCRIPT, command]
+            res = dr.run_cmd(argv, runner=runner, timeout=timeout)
+            if res.exit_code != 0:
+                tail = _redacted_tail(
+                    res.stderr or res.stdout or "", [creds.db_password]
+                )
+                raise TenantError(
+                    "CONPORT_TENANT_GATE_FAILED",
+                    f"migration gate {command} in {container_name} exited {res.exit_code}: {tail}",
+                )
+    finally:
+        with contextlib.suppress(FileNotFoundError):
+            os.unlink(tmp)
+
+
+# --- resolution -----------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def _envrc_credentials(
+    values: Optional[Mapping[str, str]],
+) -> Optional[TenantCredentials]:
+    vals = values or {}
+    if not all(str(vals.get(key) or "").strip() for key in CRED_KEYS):
+        return None
+    name, user, password = (str(vals[key]).strip() for key in CRED_KEYS)
+    return TenantCredentials(name, user, password, "legacy_envrc")
+
+
+def _main_checkout_envrc(identity: Any) -> Dict[str, str]:
+    root = getattr(identity, "project_root", None)
+    if not root:
+        return {}
+    try:
+        return dict(load_envrc(Path(str(root)) / ENVRC_FILENAME).values)
+    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
+        return {}
+
+
+def _legacy_shared(evidence: str) -> TenantCredentials:
+    return TenantCredentials(SHARED_DB, "", "", "legacy_shared", evidence=evidence)
+
+
+def _with_path(exc: TenantError, path: Path) -> TenantError:
+    detail = (
+        f"{exc.detail}; secrets file {path}" if exc.detail else f"secrets file {path}"
+    )
+    return TenantError(exc.code, detail)
+
+
+def _current(path: Path) -> Optional[TenantCredentials]:
+    creds = load_secrets(path)
+    if creds is None:
+        return None
+    state = _read_state(path)
+    owed = state == STATE_GATE or _database_progress(state) is not None
+    return replace(creds, gate_pending=owed)
+
+
+def _record_migration(path: Path) -> Callable[[str], None]:
+    return lambda name: _write_state(path, f"{STATE_DATABASE}:{name}")
+
+
+def _resume_locked(identity: Any, path: Path, psql: PsqlRunner) -> TenantCredentials:
+    """Finish an interrupted database phase with the password already on disk."""
+    creds = load_secrets(path)
+    if creds is None:
+        raise TenantError(
+            "CONPORT_TENANT_SECRETS_INVALID", f"{path} vanished during provisioning"
+        )
+    if not creds.db_name.startswith("conport_"):
+        raise TenantError(
+            "CONPORT_TENANT_PROVISION_FAILED", f"{path} names no provisioned tenant"
+        )
+    progress = _database_progress(_read_state(path)) or ""
+    try:
+        provision_database(
+            slug=creds.db_name[len("conport_") :],
+            workspace_id=str(identity.project_root),
+            password=creds.db_password,
+            psql=psql,
+            schema_path=schema_path(),
+            migrations_dir=migrations_dir(),
+            resume=True,
+            applied_through=progress,
+            on_migration=_record_migration(path),
+        )
+    except TenantError as exc:
+        raise _with_path(exc, path) from None
+    _write_state(path, STATE_GATE)
+    return replace(creds, source="provisioned", gate_pending=True)
+
+
+def _provision_locked(identity: Any, path: Path, psql: PsqlRunner) -> TenantCredentials:
+    slug = tenant_slug(identity)
+    password = secrets.token_hex(24)
+    creds = TenantCredentials(
+        f"conport_{slug}", f"conport_{slug}", password, "provisioned", gate_pending=True
+    )
+
+    def persist() -> None:
+        _write_state(
+            path, STATE_DATABASE
+        )  # before the secrets: a crash in between re-provisions
+        write_secrets(path, creds)
+
+    try:
+        provision_database(
+            slug=slug,
+            workspace_id=str(identity.project_root),
+            password=password,
+            psql=psql,
+            schema_path=schema_path(),
+            migrations_dir=migrations_dir(),
+            persist=persist,
+            on_migration=_record_migration(path),
+        )
+    except TenantError as exc:
+        raise _with_path(exc, path) from None
+    _write_state(path, STATE_GATE)
+    return creds
+
+
+def resolve_tenant(
+    identity: Any,
+    *,
+    envrc_values: Optional[Mapping[str, str]],
+    psql: PsqlRunner,
+    secrets_root: Optional[Path] = None,
+    provision: bool = True,
+) -> Optional[TenantCredentials]:
+    """Secrets file → legacy envrc → shared store with history → provision.
+
+    ``provision=False`` (doctor) never writes and never provisions: it returns
+    None where a start would provision. An unavailable legacy row count raises
+    ``CONPORT_TENANT_UNKNOWN_HISTORY`` either way. ``identity`` is the lifecycle's own
+    identity object (``project_id``, ``project_root``, ``project_hash``).
+    """
+    project_id = str(getattr(identity, "project_id", "") or "")
+    if not project_id:
+        raise TenantError(
+            "PROJECT_IDENTITY_UNKNOWN", "conport tenant needs a git project identity"
+        )
+    path = secrets_path(project_id, secrets_root=secrets_root)
+
+    current = _current(path)
+    if current is not None:
+        if _database_progress(_read_state(path)) is None:
+            return current
+        if not provision:
+            return replace(current, evidence="tenant provisioning incomplete")
+        with provision_lock(project_id):
+            if _database_progress(_read_state(path)) is None:
+                return _current(path) or current
+            return _resume_locked(identity, path, psql)
+
+    adopted = _envrc_credentials(envrc_values) or _envrc_credentials(
+        _main_checkout_envrc(identity)
+    )
+    if adopted is not None:
+        if not provision:
+            return adopted
+        with provision_lock(project_id):
+            existing = _current(path)
+            if existing is not None:
+                return existing
+            write_secrets(path, adopted)
+            _clear_state(path)
+        return adopted
+
+    count = count_legacy_rows(legacy_workspace_aliases(identity), psql=psql)
+    if count is None:
+        # Unknown is not "has history": guessing either way could split a project's
+        # data between the shared store and a new tenant (final review R2).
+        raise TenantError(
+            UNKNOWN_HISTORY,
+            f"{UNREACHABLE_EVIDENCE}: this project's history in the shared {SHARED_DB} "
+            f"store is unknown; start Postgres ({AGE_CONTAINER}) or fix docker, then "
+            "retry; nothing was changed",
+        )
+    if count > 0:
+        return _legacy_shared(
+            f"{count} rows for this project in the shared {SHARED_DB} store"
+        )
+    if not provision:
+        return None
+    with provision_lock(project_id):
+        existing = _current(path)  # another launcher may have provisioned meanwhile
+        if existing is not None:
+            if _database_progress(_read_state(path)) is not None:
+                return _resume_locked(identity, path, psql)
+            return existing
+        return _provision_locked(identity, path, psql)

+def inspect_all_managed_containers(
+    *,
+    runner: Optional[Runner] = None,
+    timeout: float = 25.0,
+) -> DockerInspectResult:
+    """List every dopemux-managed container, stopped ones included (read-only).
+
+    ``docker ps -a --filter label=dopemux.managed=true``: reap must see exited
+    containers too, so a stopped sidecar does not keep a removed worktree's
+    leases alive.
+    """
+    run = runner or subprocess.run
+    if runner is None and shutil.which("docker") is None:
+        return DockerInspectResult(available=False, error="docker binary not found on PATH")
+
+    try:
+        result = run(
+            [
+                "docker",
+                "ps",
+                "-a",
+                "--filter",
+                "label=dopemux.managed=true",
+                "--format",
+                "{{json .}}",
+            ],
+            capture_output=True,
+            text=True,
+            timeout=timeout,
+            check=False,
+        )
+    except FileNotFoundError:
+        return DockerInspectResult(available=False, error="docker binary not found")
+    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
+        return DockerInspectResult(available=False, error="docker ps -a timed out")
+    except OSError as exc:
+        return DockerInspectResult(available=False, error=f"docker ps -a failed: {exc}")
+
+    if result.returncode != 0:
+        err = (result.stderr or result.stdout or "docker ps -a failed").strip()
+        return DockerInspectResult(available=False, error=err[:500])
+
+    return DockerInspectResult(
+        available=True, containers=parse_docker_ps_json_lines(result.stdout or "")
+    )
+

 def classify_container_ownership(
     container: DockerContainerInfo,

-def compose_image_name(project_name: str, service: str) -> str:
-    """Compose v2 default image name for a service with `build:` and no `image:`."""
-    return f"{project_name}-{service}"
+#: Dockerfile of each compose-built sidecar (compose.yml ``build.dockerfile``, context ``.``).
+SIDECAR_DOCKERFILES: Dict[str, str] = {
+    "conport": "docker/mcp-servers/conport/Dockerfile",
+    "dope-memory": "services/working-memory-assistant/Dockerfile.dope-memory",
+}
+BUILD_OVERRIDE_FILENAME = "compose.build.yml"
+_IMAGE_KEY_LEN = 12
+
+
+def image_source_key(service: str, product: Path) -> str:
+    """Short hash of the product root path and the service's Dockerfile bytes.
+
+    Changing the Dockerfile changes the key, so the next start builds a new image.
+    An unreadable Dockerfile hashes as a marker (``compose build`` then fails on it).
+    """
+    digest = hashlib.sha256(str(Path(product).resolve()).encode("utf-8") + b"\0")
+    rel = SIDECAR_DOCKERFILES.get(service)
+    if rel:
+        try:
+            digest.update((Path(product) / rel).read_bytes())
+        except OSError:
+            digest.update(b"\0dockerfile-unreadable")
+    return digest.hexdigest()[:_IMAGE_KEY_LEN]
+
+
+def compose_image_name(service: str, *, product: Optional[Path] = None) -> str:
+    """``dopemux-<service>:<key>``: one image per service and Dockerfile version (design D4).
+
+    Every worktree and project built from the same product checkout shares it, so
+    only the first launch on a host (per Dockerfile version) builds; ``<key>`` is
+    :func:`image_source_key`. Sidecar source edits that leave the Dockerfile
+    unchanged need a forced rebuild (``docker image rm`` the tag). The compose
+    override and the build override set it as ``image:``, so ``compose build`` tags
+    it and ``compose up`` runs it.
+    """
+    root = Path(product) if product is not None else product_root()
+    svc = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_.-]+", "-", service.lower())
+    return f"dopemux-{svc}:{image_source_key(service, root)}"
+
+
+def render_build_override(product: Path) -> str:
+    """Compose override for ``compose build``: the shared ``image:`` tag of each sidecar."""
+    lines = ["# Generated by dopemux: shared image tags for `compose build` (design D4).", "services:"]
+    for svc in SIDECAR_DOCKERFILES:
+        lines.append(f"  {svc}:")
+        lines.append(f"    image: {compose_image_name(svc, product=product)}")
+    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
+
+
+def compose_build_inputs(
+    runtime_dir: Path, *, product: Optional[Path] = None
+) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
+    """``(build override, env file)`` for ``compose build`` of a sidecar image.
+
+    The build override (rewritten each time) only sets the shared ``image:`` tags;
+    the env file is the lifecycle's ``mcp.env`` or an empty placeholder (compose
+    needs ``--env-file`` to exist; it does not affect the image). Shared by the
+    launcher's detached build and ``mcp init``'s pre-build.
+    """
+    runtime_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    override = runtime_dir / BUILD_OVERRIDE_FILENAME
+    env_file = runtime_dir / "mcp.env"
+    root = Path(product) if product is not None else product_root()
+    override.write_text(render_build_override(root), encoding="utf-8")
+    if not env_file.exists():
+        env_file.write_text("", encoding="utf-8")
+    return override, env_file

+_SPEC_ORIGIN_CODE = (
+    "import importlib.util as u; s = u.find_spec('dopemux'); "
+    "print(s.origin if s and s.origin else '')"
+)
+
+
+def _probe_dopemux_file(bin_path: str) -> Optional[str]:
+    """``dopemux.__file__`` as the interpreter behind the ``bin_path`` script sees it.
+
+    Reads the script's shebang and asks that interpreter (isolated mode, no
+    package import) where ``dopemux`` resolves. None when unknown.
+    """
+    try:
+        with open(bin_path, "rb") as fh:
+            first = fh.readline(1024)
+    except OSError:
+        return None
+    if not first.startswith(b"#!"):
+        return None
+    parts = first[2:].decode("utf-8", "replace").split()
+    if not parts:
+        return None
+    interpreter = parts[0]
+    if Path(interpreter).name == "env" and len(parts) > 1:
+        interpreter = shutil.which(parts[-1]) or parts[-1]
+    try:
+        proc = subprocess.run(
+            [interpreter, "-I", "-c", _SPEC_ORIGIN_CODE],
+            capture_output=True,
+            text=True,
+            timeout=10,
+            check=False,
+        )
+    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
+        return None
+    origin = proc.stdout.strip()
+    return origin if proc.returncode == 0 and origin else None
+
+
+def _check_dopemux_bin(findings: List[Finding]) -> None:
+    """D1: launcher entries run ``${DOPEMUX_BIN:-dopemux}``; PATH's copy must be this product."""
+    bin_path = shutil.which("dopemux")
+    if not bin_path:
+        return
+    origin = _probe_dopemux_file(bin_path)
+    if not origin:
+        return
+    from .docker_runtime import product_root
+
+    try:
+        product = product_root().resolve()
+    except FileNotFoundError:
+        return
+    try:
+        Path(origin).resolve().relative_to(product)
+        return
+    except ValueError:
+        pass
+    _add(
+        findings,
+        "DOPEMUX_BIN_NOT_EDITABLE",
+        "WARN",
+        (
+            f"`dopemux` on PATH ({bin_path}) loads dopemux from {origin}, not from this "
+            f"checkout {product}; .mcp.json launcher entries would run that copy"
+        ),
+        evidence=[bin_path, origin],
+        recommendation=f"uv tool install --editable {product}",
+    )
+

+def _check_conport_tenant(
+    findings: List[Finding],
+    identity: Any,
+    envrc_values: Mapping[str, str],

+    """D5, read-only: which ConPort tenant a start would use (never writes, never provisions)."""
+    from . import conport_tenant as ct
+

+        return

+        creds = ct.resolve_tenant(
+            identity,
+            envrc_values=envrc_values,
+            psql=psql or ct.default_psql_runner(),
+            provision=False,
+        )
+    except ct.TenantError as exc:
+        if exc.code == ct.UNKNOWN_HISTORY:
+            _add(

+                "CONPORT_TENANT_UNVERIFIED",

+                    f"state refuses conport with {ct.UNKNOWN_HISTORY} and changes nothing; "
+                    "check that dopemux-postgres-age is running and re-run the doctor."
+                ),
+            )
+            return
+        _add(
+            findings,
+            exc.code,
+            "FAIL",
+            exc.detail or exc.code,
+            service="conport",
+            recommendation=(
+                "Restore the ConPort tenant file named above from backup, or ask the operator."
+            ),
+        )
+        return
+    except (OSError, ValueError):
+        return
+    if creds is None:  # a start would provision a fresh tenant (R1: no finding)
+        return
+    if creds.source == "legacy_shared":
+        _add(

+            "CONPORT_LEGACY_SHARED_STORE",
+            "WARN",

+                f"({creds.evidence}); it has no project wall yet"

+            recommendation=(
+                f"No action needed now: this project keeps the shared {ct.SHARED_DB} "
+                "store; migrating its rows to a project tenant is M2."
+            ),
+        )

+def catalog_client_shape(spec: dict[str, Any]) -> Literal["launcher", "direct"]:
+    """How a client connects to a catalog entry.
+
+    `client: launcher` means clients get a stdio `dopemux mcp launch <name>`
+    entry that resolves the sidecar at connect time; the entry's `transport`
+    and `url_template` then describe the sidecar, not the client. Absent (or
+    `direct`) means the client connects to `url`/`url_template` itself.
+    """
+    client = spec.get("client", "direct")
+    if client not in {"launcher", "direct"}:
+        raise MCPFleetCatalogError(f"unsupported catalog `client` value: {client!r}")
+    return "launcher" if client == "launcher" else "direct"
+
+
+def expected_client_type(spec: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
+    """The `type` a client config (.mcp.json) must carry for this entry."""
+    if catalog_client_shape(spec) == "launcher":
+        return "stdio"
+    return str(spec["transport"])
+

+"""``dopemux mcp launch <service>``: find or start the worktree's sidecar, then proxy stdio.
+
+Design D2/D4/D7 of claudedocs/mcp-just-works-design-2026-11-05.md:
+
+1. Root: git top-level of ``--root`` or the process cwd; refuse outside git or
+   when ``.mcp.json`` is not the dopemux-rendered launcher entry.
+2. Identity from git only (``resolve_identity_view``), never ambient env.
+3. Warm path: registry record + SDK probe, no lifecycle call. Cold path: under
+   ``instance_lock(runtime_dir)``, start through ``run_lifecycle("start")``.
+   A missing image is built detached and the launch refuses (never block a
+   session on a multi-minute build).
+4. Task Orchestrator: under ``reserved_port_lock(7890)``, ownership is proven
+   from the labels of the container publishing 7890; foreign or unknown
+   owners refuse, a free port starts this project's jar.
+5. Attach record, then the SDK stdio proxy with an owner guard that re-proves
+   the backend's owner from Docker labels before every backend connect (the
+   first included; this launch-time check's reserved lock is released by then)
+   and again right after each ``initialize``.
+
+Every refusal is one line ``CODE: remedy`` on stderr and exit code 3 (an
+``OSError`` before the proxy runs is ``LAUNCH_IO_ERROR``). SIGTERM and SIGHUP
+exit 143 and 129 through ``finally``, so the attach record is always detached.
+
+``launch(service, *, root=None, env=None, registry_path=None, reaper=...)``:
+``env=None`` means ``os.environ`` read at call time (a default of the mapping
+itself would render every variable, secrets included, in ``help()``).
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import contextlib
+import json
+import os
+import signal
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import threading
+import time
+from dataclasses import dataclass, field
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any, Callable, Dict, Iterator, List, Mapping, Optional, Sequence
+from urllib.parse import urlparse
+
+from . import conport_tenant as ct
+from . import docker_runtime as dr
+from . import stdio_proxy
+from .docker_inspect import DockerContainerInfo, inspect_running_containers
+from .lifecycle import _port_is_free, run_lifecycle, runtime_dir_for
+from .locks import instance_lock, reserved_port_lock
+from .reap import _pid_alive, attach, detach, maybe_reap
+from .runtime_registry import RuntimeRegistry, default_registry_path
+from .runtime_state import (
+    PROJECT_MCP_FILENAME,
+    ProjectIdentityView,
+    resolve_identity_view,
+    sidecar_url,
+)
+from .stdio_proxy import (
+    BackendSpec,
+    ProxyRefused,
+    probe_backend,
+    quiet_sdk_loggers,
+    run_proxy,
+)
+from .task_orchestrator_identity import identity_from_docker_labels, match_target
+
+__all__ = [
+    "LaunchError",
+    "LaunchTarget",
+    "attach",
+    "build_target",
+    "detach",
+    "ensure_backend",
+    "ensure_orchestrator",
+    "find_backend",
+    "launch",
+    "reconnect_guard",
+    "resolve_launch_root",
+]
+
+DEFAULT_WAIT_S = 25.0
+WAIT_ENV = "DOPEMUX_LAUNCH_WAIT"
+POLL_INTERVAL_S = 0.5
+EXIT_REFUSED = 3
+
+TO_SERVICE = "task-orchestrator"
+TO_PORT = 7890
+TO_BACKEND = BackendSpec(f"http://127.0.0.1:{TO_PORT}/mcp", "http", TO_SERVICE)
+TO_WRONG_PROJECT = "TASK_ORCHESTRATOR_START_BLOCKED_WRONG_PROJECT"
+TO_UNKNOWN_OWNER = "TASK_ORCHESTRATOR_START_BLOCKED_UNKNOWN_OWNER"
+OWNER_CHANGED = "LAUNCH_BACKEND_OWNER_CHANGED"
+#: ``docker ps`` budget of one reconnect-guard call (the proxy bounds the call itself too).
+GUARD_DOCKER_TIMEOUT_S = 10.0
+
+Probe = Callable[[BackendSpec], bool]
+
+
+class LaunchError(Exception):
+    """A refusal with a finding code and a one-line remedy."""
+
+    def __init__(self, code: str, remedy: str) -> None:
+        super().__init__(f"{code}: {remedy}")
+        self.code = code
+        self.remedy = remedy
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class LaunchTarget:
+    root: Path
+    identity: ProjectIdentityView
+    service: str
+    spec: Mapping[str, Any]
+    runtime_dir: Path
+    compose_project: str
+    # Carried for the lifecycle call and registry reads; not part of identity.
+    catalog: Mapping[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict, repr=False, compare=False)
+    registry_path: Optional[Path] = None
+
+
+# --- root -------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def _git_toplevel(base: Path) -> Optional[Path]:
+    if not base.is_dir():
+        return None
+    try:
+        proc = subprocess.run(
+            ["git", "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"],
+            cwd=base,
+            capture_output=True,
+            text=True,
+            timeout=5,
+            check=False,
+        )
+    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
+        return None
+    top = proc.stdout.strip()
+    if proc.returncode != 0 or not top:
+        return None
+    return Path(top).resolve()
+
+
+def _is_launcher_entry(name: str, entry: Any) -> bool:
+    return (
+        isinstance(entry, dict)
+        and entry.get("type") == "stdio"
+        and entry.get("args") == ["mcp", "launch", name]
+    )
+
+
+def _require_managed_mcp_json(root: Path, service: Optional[str]) -> None:
+    path = root / PROJECT_MCP_FILENAME
+    remedy = f"{path} is not dopemux-rendered; run dopemux mcp repair-config --apply"
+    try:
+        data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    except (OSError, ValueError):
+        raise LaunchError("LAUNCH_MCP_JSON_NOT_MANAGED", remedy) from None
+    servers = data.get("mcpServers") if isinstance(data, dict) else None
+    if not isinstance(servers, dict):
+        raise LaunchError("LAUNCH_MCP_JSON_NOT_MANAGED", remedy)
+    if service is not None:
+        managed = _is_launcher_entry(service, servers.get(service))
+    else:
+        managed = any(
+            _is_launcher_entry(name, entry) for name, entry in servers.items()
+        )
+    if not managed:
+        raise LaunchError("LAUNCH_MCP_JSON_NOT_MANAGED", remedy)
+
+
+def resolve_launch_root(
+    explicit: Optional[Path], *, cwd: Path, service: Optional[str] = None
+) -> Path:
+    """Git top-level of ``explicit or cwd`` whose ``.mcp.json`` is dopemux-rendered.
+
+    With ``service``, that service's entry must be the stdio launcher entry
+    Task 3a renders; without it, at least one entry must be.
+    """
+    base = Path(explicit if explicit is not None else cwd).expanduser()
+    root = _git_toplevel(base)
+    if root is None:
+        raise LaunchError(
+            "LAUNCH_ROOT_NOT_GIT",
+            f"{base} is not inside a git work tree; start the session in a checkout",
+        )
+    _require_managed_mcp_json(root, service)
+    return root
+
+
+# --- target -------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def build_target(
+    root: Path,
+    service: str,
+    *,
+    catalog: Mapping[str, Any],
+    registry_path: Optional[Path] = None,
+) -> LaunchTarget:
+    from .fleet_catalog import catalog_client_shape  # lazy: imports the CLI module
+
+    spec = (catalog.get("servers") or {}).get(service)
+    if not isinstance(spec, Mapping) or catalog_client_shape(dict(spec)) != "launcher":
+        raise LaunchError(
+            "LAUNCH_SERVICE_UNKNOWN",
+            f"{service} is not a launcher service in the MCP catalog; run dopemux mcp doctor",
+        )
+    identity = resolve_identity_view(Path(root))
+    if (
+        not identity.project_id
+        or not identity.project_root
+        or identity.identity_confidence == "UNKNOWN"
+    ):
+        raise LaunchError("PROJECT_IDENTITY_UNKNOWN", "run dopemux mcp doctor")
+    slug = dr.project_slug(Path(identity.project_root).name)
+    return LaunchTarget(
+        root=Path(root),
+        identity=identity,
+        service=service,
+        spec=spec,
+        runtime_dir=runtime_dir_for(identity, registry_path=registry_path),
+        compose_project=dr.compose_project_name(slug, identity.worktree_hash or ""),
+        catalog=catalog,
+        registry_path=registry_path,
+    )
+
+
+# --- backend discovery --------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def _registry_file(target: LaunchTarget, registry_path: Optional[Path] = None) -> Path:
+    return Path(registry_path or target.registry_path or default_registry_path())
+
+
+def _backend(target: LaunchTarget, ports: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Optional[BackendSpec]:
+    """The sidecar at ``ports``; None when they lack the service's port (never a default)."""
+    clean = {
+        str(k): int(v)
+        for k, v in ports.items()
+        if isinstance(v, int) or str(v).isdigit()
+    }
+    url = sidecar_url(target.spec, clean)
+    if url is None:
+        return None
+    return BackendSpec(url, target.spec["transport"], target.service)
+
+
+def find_backend(
+    target: LaunchTarget, *, registry_path: Optional[Path] = None
+) -> Optional[BackendSpec]:
+    """The newest ``running`` registry record for this worktree and service.
+
+    A record without the service's port is not a backend (the cold path starts
+    through the lifecycle, which records the ports again).
+    """
+    registry = RuntimeRegistry.load(_registry_file(target, registry_path))
+    if registry.parse_status != "OK":
+        return None
+    running = [
+        rec
+        for rec in registry.find(
+            project_id=target.identity.project_id,
+            worktree_root=target.identity.worktree_root,
+            service=target.service,
+        )
+        if rec.get("status") == "running"
+    ]
+    if not running:
+        return None
+    newest = max(running, key=lambda rec: str(rec.get("updated_at") or ""))
+    return _backend(target, newest.get("ports") or {})
+
+
+def _lifecycle_env(
+    env: Optional[Mapping[str, str]], catalog: Mapping[str, Any]
+) -> Dict[str, str]:
+    """Process env for the lifecycle minus ambient identity and catalog port vars.
+
+    Ports come from the lease registry (and the checkout's own envrc, which
+    the lifecycle reads itself), never from another checkout's exports. The
+    identity/port/tenant set is ``docker_runtime.AMBIENT_SCRUB_VARS`` (D3, D5),
+    defined once and shared with the lifecycle's compose environment.
+    """
+    drop = set(dr.AMBIENT_SCRUB_VARS)
+    for spec in (catalog.get("servers") or {}).values():
+        if not isinstance(spec, Mapping):
+            continue
+        if spec.get("port_var"):
+            drop.add(str(spec["port_var"]))
+        for extra in spec.get("extra_port_vars") or []:
+            if isinstance(extra, Mapping) and extra.get("var"):
+                drop.add(str(extra["var"]))
+    source = os.environ if env is None else env
+    return {k: v for k, v in source.items() if k not in drop}
+
+
+#: Env keys whose values are secrets (same markers as the generated mcp.env filter).
+_SECRET_KEY_MARKERS = ("PASSWORD", "SECRET", "TOKEN", "API_KEY", "CREDENTIAL")
+
+
+def _known_secrets(target: LaunchTarget, env: Optional[Mapping[str, str]]) -> List[str]:
+    """Secret values a start failure could echo: the tenant password and secret env."""
+    found: List[str] = []
+    try:
+        creds = ct.load_secrets(ct.secrets_path(str(target.identity.project_id or "")))
+    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - no or unreadable secrets file: nothing to add
+        creds = None
+    if creds is not None and creds.db_password:
+        found.append(creds.db_password)
+    for key, value in (os.environ if env is None else env).items():
+        if (
+            value
+            and len(value) >= 4
+            and any(m in key.upper() for m in _SECRET_KEY_MARKERS)
+        ):
+            found.append(value)
+    return found
+
+
+def _lifecycle_error(
+    result: Any, service: str, secrets: Sequence[str] = ()
+) -> LaunchError:
+    """The refusal for a failed lifecycle start; every echoed text is redacted first."""
+
+    def scrub(text: str, limit: Optional[int] = None) -> str:
+        return ct.redacted_tail(text, secrets, limit=limit)
+
+    for finding in getattr(result, "blocking_findings", None) or []:
+        if finding.get("code"):
+            message = scrub(finding.get("message") or "start blocked")
+            return LaunchError(
+                str(finding["code"]), f"{message}; run dopemux mcp doctor"
+            )
+    for svc in getattr(result, "services", None) or []:
+        for finding in svc.get("findings") or []:
+            if finding.get("severity") in {"ERROR", "FAIL"} and finding.get("code"):
+                return LaunchError(
+                    str(finding["code"]), scrub(str(finding.get("message") or ""))
+                )
+    tail = ""
+    for svc in getattr(result, "services", None) or []:
+        stderr = ((svc.get("result") or {}).get("stderr") or "").strip()
+        if stderr:
+            tail = f" ({scrub(stderr, limit=200)})"
+    return LaunchError(
+        "LAUNCH_START_FAILED",
+        f"dopemux mcp start --services {service} exited {getattr(result, 'exit_code', '?')}"
+        f"{tail}; run dopemux mcp doctor",
+    )
+
+
+def _backend_from_result(target: LaunchTarget, result: Any) -> Optional[BackendSpec]:
+    for svc in getattr(result, "services", None) or []:
+        if svc.get("service") == target.service and isinstance(
+            svc.get("ports"), Mapping
+        ):
+            backend = _backend(target, svc["ports"])
+            if backend is None:
+                raise LaunchError(
+                    "LAUNCH_BACKEND_PORT_UNKNOWN",
+                    f"start reported no {target.spec.get('port_var') or 'port'} for "
+                    f"{target.service}; run dopemux mcp doctor",
+                )
+            return backend
+    return None
+
+
+def _wait_for(
+    backend: BackendSpec,
+    *,
+    probe: Probe,
+    deadline: float,
+    clock: Callable[[], float],
+    sleep: Callable[[float], None],
+) -> BackendSpec:
+    while clock() < deadline:
+        sleep(POLL_INTERVAL_S)
+        if probe(backend):
+            return backend
+    raise LaunchError("LAUNCH_WAIT_EXCEEDED", "run /mcp reconnect all")
+
+
+# --- image policy (D4) ----------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def build_pid_path(runtime_dir: Path, service: str) -> Path:
+    """``<runtime_dir>/build-<service>.pid``: one per service, so both sidecars may build at once."""
+    return Path(runtime_dir) / f"build-{service}.pid"
+
+
+def _live_build_pid(pid_file: Path) -> Optional[int]:
+    """The pid of a build still running, or None (absent, dead, or older than the build budget).
+
+    The age limit is ``build_timeout_secs()`` (``DOPEMUX_MCP_BUILD_TIMEOUT_SECS``,
+    default 1800 s), the budget a build gets, never the shorter ``up`` budget.
+    """
+    try:
+        pid = int(pid_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip())
+        age = time.time() - pid_file.stat().st_mtime
+    except (OSError, ValueError):
+        return None
+    if pid <= 0 or age > dr.build_timeout_secs() or not _pid_alive(pid):
+        return None  # stale: replaced by the next build
+    return pid
+
+
+def _write_build_pid(pid_file: Path, pid: int) -> None:
+    tmp = pid_file.with_name(f".{pid_file.name}.{os.getpid()}.tmp")
+    tmp.write_text(f"{pid}\n", encoding="utf-8")
+    os.replace(tmp, pid_file)
+
+
+def _build_image_if_missing(target: LaunchTarget) -> None:
+    """Refuse with ``LAUNCH_IMAGE_BUILDING`` while the shared image is missing (D4).
+
+    The first launch spawns one detached ``compose build`` of this service and
+    records its pid in ``<runtime_dir>/build-<service>.pid``; a launch of the same
+    service while that pid is alive spawns nothing. The other service's build is
+    independent: conport and dope-memory may build at once.
+    """
+    if target.spec.get("management_model") != "compose-service":
+        return
+    product = dr.product_root()
+    env = dr.env_with_compose_interpolation(product=product, scrub_ambient=True)
+    image = dr.compose_image_name(target.service, product=product)
+    if dr.docker_image_exists(image, env=env) is not False:
+        return
+    remedy = "building image; retry with /mcp reconnect all in a few minutes"
+    log_path = target.runtime_dir / "build.log"
+    pid_file = build_pid_path(target.runtime_dir, target.service)
+    running = _live_build_pid(pid_file)
+    if running is not None:
+        print(
+            f"dopemux mcp launch: {image} is still building (pid {running}); log {log_path}",
+            file=sys.stderr,
+        )
+        raise LaunchError("LAUNCH_IMAGE_BUILDING", remedy)
+    override, env_file = dr.compose_build_inputs(target.runtime_dir, product=product)
+    command = dr.compose_build_command(
+        product=product,
+        override_path=override,
+        env_file=env_file,
+        project_name=target.compose_project,
+        services=[target.service],
+    )
+    # Append: conport and dope-memory may build at once (separate pid files).
+    with open(log_path, "ab") as log:
+        proc = subprocess.Popen(
+            command,
+            stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
+            stdout=log,
+            stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
+            cwd=str(product),
+            env=env,
+            start_new_session=True,
+        )
+    _write_build_pid(pid_file, proc.pid)
+    print(f"dopemux mcp launch: building {image}; log {log_path}", file=sys.stderr)
+    raise LaunchError("LAUNCH_IMAGE_BUILDING", remedy)
+
+
+# --- ensure ---------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def ensure_backend(
+    target: LaunchTarget,
+    *,
+    wait_s: float,
+    lifecycle: Callable[..., Any] = run_lifecycle,
+    probe: Probe = probe_backend,
+    clock: Callable[[], float] = time.monotonic,
+    sleep: Callable[[float], None] = time.sleep,
+    env: Optional[Mapping[str, str]] = None,
+) -> BackendSpec:
+    """A reachable sidecar for ``target`` (conport / dope-memory)."""
+    deadline = clock() + wait_s
+    warm = find_backend(target)
+    if warm is not None and probe(warm):
+        return warm
+    with instance_lock(target.runtime_dir):
+        warm = find_backend(target)
+        if warm is not None and probe(warm):
+            return warm
+        _build_image_if_missing(target)
+        result = lifecycle(
+            "start",
+            repo=target.root,
+            services=[target.service],
+            catalog=target.catalog,
+            registry_path=target.registry_path,
+            process_env=_lifecycle_env(env, target.catalog),
+            trust_env=False,
+        )
+        if result.exit_code != 0:
+            raise _lifecycle_error(result, target.service, _known_secrets(target, env))
+        backend = _backend_from_result(target, result) or find_backend(target)
+        if backend is None:
+            raise LaunchError(
+                "LAUNCH_START_FAILED",
+                f"start reported no ports for {target.service}; run dopemux mcp doctor",
+            )
+        if probe(backend):
+            return backend
+    return _wait_for(backend, probe=probe, deadline=deadline, clock=clock, sleep=sleep)
+
+
+def _orchestrator_owner_verdict(
+    owners: List[DockerContainerInfo], target: LaunchTarget
+) -> None:
+    """Return when the 7890 container is this project's; raise otherwise."""
+    if len(owners) != 1:
+        raise LaunchError(TO_UNKNOWN_OWNER, "run dopemux mcp doctor")
+    owner = owners[0]
+    observed = identity_from_docker_labels(
+        owner.labels or {}, port=TO_PORT, container_name=owner.name
+    )
+    verdict = match_target(
+        observed,
+        project_id=target.identity.project_id,
+        project_root=target.identity.project_root,
+    )
+    if verdict == "OK":
+        return
+    if verdict == "WRONG_PROJECT":
+        owner_root = observed.project_root or observed.project_id or owner.name
+        raise LaunchError(
+            TO_WRONG_PROJECT,
+            f"{owner_root}; run dopemux mcp switch-project here to take it over",
+        )
+    raise LaunchError(TO_UNKNOWN_OWNER, "run dopemux mcp doctor")
+
+
+def _orchestrator_port_is_ours(
+    target: LaunchTarget, state: Any, port_is_free: Callable[[int], bool]
+) -> bool:
+    """True when this project's orchestrator publishes :7890, False when the port is free.
+
+    Raises ``LaunchError`` for a foreign or unknown owner.
+    """
+    owners = (
+        [c for c in state.containers if TO_PORT in (c.published_ports or [])]
+        if state.available
+        else []
+    )
+    if owners:
+        _orchestrator_owner_verdict(owners, target)
+        return True
+    if not port_is_free(TO_PORT):
+        raise LaunchError(TO_UNKNOWN_OWNER, "run dopemux mcp doctor")
+    return False
+
+
+def ensure_orchestrator(
+    target: LaunchTarget,
+    *,
+    docker: Callable[[], Any] = inspect_running_containers,
+    lifecycle: Callable[..., Any] = run_lifecycle,
+    probe: Probe = probe_backend,
+    port_is_free: Callable[[int], bool] = _port_is_free,
+    wait_s: float = DEFAULT_WAIT_S,
+    clock: Callable[[], float] = time.monotonic,
+    sleep: Callable[[float], None] = time.sleep,
+    env: Optional[Mapping[str, str]] = None,
+) -> BackendSpec:
+    """The reserved-port orchestrator, proven to be this project's (D2, D7)."""
+    deadline = clock() + wait_s
+    with reserved_port_lock(TO_PORT):
+        if _orchestrator_port_is_ours(target, docker(), port_is_free):
+            return TO_BACKEND
+        result = lifecycle(
+            "start",
+            repo=target.root,
+            services=[TO_SERVICE],
+            catalog=target.catalog,
+            registry_path=target.registry_path,
+            process_env=_lifecycle_env(env, target.catalog),
+            trust_env=False,
+        )
+        if result.exit_code != 0:
+            raise _lifecycle_error(result, TO_SERVICE, _known_secrets(target, env))
+        if probe(TO_BACKEND):
+            return TO_BACKEND
+    return _wait_for(
+        TO_BACKEND, probe=probe, deadline=deadline, clock=clock, sleep=sleep
+    )
+
+
+# --- reconnect guard (C1) ---------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def _check_sidecar_owner(
+    target: LaunchTarget,
+    port: Optional[int],
+    state: Any,
+    port_is_free: Callable[[int], bool],
+) -> None:
+    """Pass when ``port`` is free or published by this worktree's own sidecar; else raise."""
+    service = target.service
+    if port is None:
+        raise LaunchError(OWNER_CHANGED, f"the {service} backend URL names no port")
+    owners = (
+        [c for c in state.containers if port in (c.published_ports or [])]
+        if state.available
+        else []
+    )
+    if not owners:
+        if port_is_free(port):
+            return  # backend down: the reconnect attempt fails and retries
+        raise LaunchError(
+            OWNER_CHANGED,
+            f":{port} is held by a process that is not this worktree's {service}; "
+            "run dopemux mcp doctor",
+        )
+    labels = (owners[0].labels or {}) if len(owners) == 1 else {}
+    ident = target.identity
+    if (
+        len(owners) != 1
+        or labels.get(dr.LABEL_MANAGED) != "true"
+        or labels.get("dopemux.project_id") != ident.project_id
+        or labels.get("dopemux.worktree_hash") != ident.worktree_hash
+        or labels.get("dopemux.service", service) != service
+    ):
+        names = ", ".join(c.name for c in owners)
+        raise LaunchError(
+            OWNER_CHANGED,
+            f"{names} now publishes :{port} and is not this worktree's {service}; "
+            f"run /mcp reconnect {service}",
+        )
+
+
+def reconnect_guard(
+    target: LaunchTarget,
+    backend: BackendSpec,
+    *,
+    docker: Optional[Callable[[], Any]] = None,
+    port_is_free: Callable[[int], bool] = _port_is_free,
+) -> Callable[[], None]:
+    """The proxy's ``reconnect_guard`` for ``target``: re-prove who owns the backend port.
+
+    task-orchestrator: the docker inspection and owner verdict of
+    ``ensure_orchestrator`` (``TASK_ORCHESTRATOR_START_BLOCKED_*``). conport and
+    dope-memory: the container publishing the backend port must carry
+    ``dopemux.managed=true`` and this instance's ``dopemux.project_id`` and
+    ``dopemux.worktree_hash`` (``LAUNCH_BACKEND_OWNER_CHANGED``). A free port passes:
+    nothing can answer there, so the connect attempt fails and is retried. The
+    proxy calls it before every connect and again after every ``initialize``.
+    """
+
+    def inspect() -> Any:
+        if docker is not None:
+            return docker()
+        return inspect_running_containers(timeout=GUARD_DOCKER_TIMEOUT_S)
+
+    port = urlparse(backend.url).port
+
+    def guard() -> None:
+        try:
+            if target.service == TO_SERVICE:
+                _orchestrator_port_is_ours(target, inspect(), port_is_free)
+            else:
+                _check_sidecar_owner(target, port, inspect(), port_is_free)
+        except LaunchError as exc:
+            raise ProxyRefused(exc.code, exc.remedy) from None
+
+    return guard
+
+
+# --- launch -----------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def _load_catalog(root: Optional[Path] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+    """The MCP catalog for ``root``: ``$DOPEMUX_MCP_CATALOG``, else its repo-local one, else bundled."""
+    import click
+
+    from dopemux.commands.mcp_commands import _load_catalog as load
+
+    try:
+        catalog: Dict[str, Any] = load(repo=root)
+    except click.ClickException as exc:
+        raise LaunchError("LAUNCH_CATALOG_INVALID", exc.format_message()) from None
+    return catalog
+
+
+# A launcher runs inside Claude's connect budget: one candidate, short docker calls
+# (each also capped at the time left), all within 8 s.
+LAUNCH_REAP_LIMITS: Dict[str, Any] = {
+    "max_candidates": 1,
+    "stop_time_s": 2,
+    "cmd_timeout_s": 3.0,
+    "deadline_s": 8.0,
+}
+
+#: Exit codes of a launcher ended by a signal (128 + signal number).
+_SIGNAL_EXITS = {"SIGTERM": 143, "SIGHUP": 129}
+
+
+@contextlib.contextmanager
+def _signals_exit_through_finally() -> Iterator[None]:
+    """SIGTERM/SIGHUP end the launch with 143/129 through ``finally``; restored on exit.
+
+    Outside an event loop the handler raises ``SystemExit(128 + signum)``. Inside
+    one (a backend probe or the proxy) raising there is unsafe (an exception group,
+    or a loop shutdown that never finishes), so the handler defers: the loop is
+    cancelled at a safe point and ``run_proxy`` returns the code, or
+    ``probe_backend`` raises it once its loop has ended. Only the main thread can
+    install handlers; elsewhere this does nothing.
+    """
+    installed: Dict[int, Any] = {}
+    stdio_proxy.reset_deferred_exit()
+
+    def _exit(signum: int, _frame: Any) -> None:
+        if stdio_proxy.defer_exit_in_event_loop(128 + signum):
+            return
+        raise SystemExit(128 + signum)
+
+    if threading.current_thread() is threading.main_thread():
+        for name in _SIGNAL_EXITS:
+            signum = getattr(signal, name, None)
+            if signum is not None:
+                installed[signum] = signal.signal(signum, _exit)
+    try:
+        yield
+    finally:
+        for signum, previous in installed.items():
+            signal.signal(signum, previous)
+
+
+def _run_reaper(reaper: Optional[Callable[[], Any]]) -> None:
+    try:
+        if reaper is not None:
+            reaper()
+        else:
+            maybe_reap(**LAUNCH_REAP_LIMITS)
+    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - cleanup must never block a launch
+        print(f"reap skipped: {exc}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
+
+
+def _wait_seconds(env: Mapping[str, str]) -> float:
+    try:
+        value = float(env.get(WAIT_ENV) or DEFAULT_WAIT_S)
+    except ValueError:
+        return DEFAULT_WAIT_S
+    return value if value > 0 else DEFAULT_WAIT_S
+
+
+def _process_cwd() -> Path:
+    try:
+        return Path.cwd()
+    except FileNotFoundError:
+        raise LaunchError(
+            "LAUNCH_ROOT_NOT_GIT", "the working directory no longer exists"
+        ) from None
+
+
+def launch(
+    service: str,
+    *,
+    root: Optional[Path] = None,
+    env: Optional[Mapping[str, str]] = None,
+    registry_path: Optional[Path] = None,
+    reaper: Optional[Callable[[], Any]] = None,
+) -> int:
+    """Run the launcher for ``service``; returns the process exit code.
+
+    ``env`` defaults to ``os.environ`` as it is when called. ``reaper`` defaults to
+    ``reap.maybe_reap`` (throttled, kill-switchable, capped at one candidate, 8 s
+    deadline); it runs after this launcher's attach record exists, so its own
+    instance is never reaped, and outside the instance lock. A reap failure never
+    refuses a launch. ``root`` (``--root``) picks the checkout and its catalog.
+    """
+    if env is None:
+        env = os.environ
+    record: Optional[Path] = None
+    with _signals_exit_through_finally(), quiet_sdk_loggers():
+        try:
+            # stdout is the MCP channel: nothing before the proxy may print there.
+            with contextlib.redirect_stdout(sys.stderr):
+                launch_root = resolve_launch_root(
+                    root, cwd=_process_cwd(), service=service
+                )
+                target = build_target(
+                    launch_root,
+                    service,
+                    catalog=_load_catalog(launch_root),
+                    registry_path=registry_path,
+                )
+                # D8: the attach record is written under the instance lock, so a reap
+                # that holds the lock sees either the record or none of this launch.
+                # Released before the reaper (the reap takes the lock itself) and
+                # before ensure_backend (which takes it again; it is not re-entrant).
+                with instance_lock(target.runtime_dir):
+                    record = attach(target.runtime_dir)
+                _run_reaper(reaper)
+                wait_s = _wait_seconds(env)
+                if service == TO_SERVICE:
+                    backend = ensure_orchestrator(target, wait_s=wait_s, env=env)
+                else:
+                    backend = ensure_backend(target, wait_s=wait_s, env=env)
+                guard = reconnect_guard(target, backend)
+            return run_proxy(backend, reconnect_guard=guard)
+        except LaunchError as exc:
+            print(f"{exc.code}: {exc.remedy}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
+            return EXIT_REFUSED
+        except OSError as exc:
+            # attach, build.log, a lock that never stabilised: a refusal, not a traceback
+            print(
+                f"LAUNCH_IO_ERROR: {exc}; retry, and run dopemux mcp doctor if it persists",
+                file=sys.stderr,
+                flush=True,
+            )
+            return EXIT_REFUSED
+        finally:
+            if record is not None:
+                detach(record)
